================================================================================
PROJECT DESCRIPTION:
Zed is a high-performance, multi-player code editor from the creators of Atom 
and Tree-sitter. Written in Rust, it leverages a custom GPU-accelerated UI 
framework (GPUI) to provide a near-instantaneous editing experience. It 
integrates AI-assisted coding features and collaborative workflows directly 
into its core.

FULL STACK:
- Language: Rust
- UI Framework: GPUI (Custom, GPU-accelerated)
- Indexing: Tree-sitter
- Collaboration: CRDTs / WebRTC
- Platform Support: macOS, Linux, Windows (WIP)
- Rendering: Metal / Vulkan / Direct3D
